- **checkpoint**: Saves a short checkpoint of the work, then compacts at a calm moment, and welcomes you back. /checkpoint writes .claude/checkpoint.md; a card above the prompt suggests compacting when the context is getting full and nothing is running; a new session in the same folder offers to resume from the checkpoint; a session opened again after 20+ minutes away shows what you asked and where Claude left off, built from the transcript with no model call (/checkpoint recap, /checkpoint recap off).
